2 – OPENING THE CONTAINER Technician Checker
Cut the closing straps and remove the hinges
Open the vacuum pouch and start to unroll the liferaft
Disconnect the 2 bowsing lines V bridles (shackles)
Disconnect the identification tube
Remove the 3 rings systems + pyrotechnic knives + yellow wires
Disconnect the Firing line
Completely unroll the liferaft
Unscrew the hoses and remove the cylinder sheath of the liferaft
Remove the cylinders from the container and start the servicing of the liferaft

Use the test and survey report of the standard liferafts and MES liferafts

Ed.09-2020
TEST AND SURVEY REPORT MES MIS
100 / 150 TO SR V PRIMARY LIFERAFT
~ PAGE 3 / 4 ~
5 – PACKING PRIMARY LIFERAFT Technician Checker
Liferaft tested and accessories checked
Fill in the liferaft label
Container and firing line checked
Protective cover checked and positioned as a cross in the container
Position the vacuum pouch in the container
Liferaft cylinders controlled, heads serviced and positioned in the cylinder sheath
Position the liferaft cylinders in the container
Put the firing head through the vacuum pouch and tighten the nut
Bring the liferaft into the container and attach the first fixing lines of the cylinder sheath
Connect the hoses
Bring the liferaft on top of the cylinders and attach the other fixing lines of the cylinder sheath
Connect the firing line (goes through the U bolt + yellow line - loop knot)
Inside the vacuum pouch:
Put the shackles through the vacuum pouch holes and tighten the shackle pins (use talc +
Allen key)
Position the shackles straight in the vacuum pouch
Outside the vacuum pouch:
Position the 3 ring systems + pyrotechnic knives + yellow wires (use a thin line to make the
operation easier)
Orientate the pyrotechnic knives and hold with adhesive tape + rubber bands

Ed.09-2020
TEST AND SURVEY REPORT MES MIS
100 / 150 TO SR V PRIMARY LIFERAFT
~ PAGE 4 / 4 ~
Lock the 3 ring systems in position with safety pins + zip ties (front part of the rolling bands)
Attach the equipment bags + paddles to the liferaft
Connect the identification tube line to the liferaft and to the vacuum pouch
Vacuum the liferaft
Secure the ladders to the liferaft (zip ties 9kg)
Light connected
Vacuum the liferaft
Make the 2 first folds and connect the bowsing line V bridles with a chackle (on both sides)
inside the vacuum pouch
Make sure the tether line goes underneath bowsing line V bridle
Tighten the shackle pins and protect the pin extremity with adhesive tape + coil the extra V line
Make the last folds of the liferaft (with venturi protection upside down)
Roll the liferaft towards the container
Remove the venturi caps
Open the venturi
Secure the venturi

Lock the 3 ring systems in position with safety pins + zip ties (front part of the rolling bands)
Attach the equipment bags + paddles to the liferaft
Connect the identification tube line to the liferaft and to the vacuum pouch
Vacuum the liferaft
Secure the ladders to the liferaft (zip ties 9kg)
Light connected
Vacuum the liferaft
Make the 2 first folds and connect the bowsing line V bridles with a chackle (on both sides)
inside the vacuum pouch
Make sure the tether line goes underneath bowsing line V bridle
Tighten the shackle pins and protect the pin extremity with adhesive tape + coil the extra V line
Make the last folds of the liferaft (with venturi protection upside down)
Roll the liferaft towards the container
Remove the venturi caps
Open the venturi
Secure the venturi
Check that the venturi flap covers are in correct position
Roll the venturi protection and hold in position in the middle of the container
Completely roll the liferaft in the container
Put the vacuum line (orange)
Weld the vacuum pouch
Connect the bowsing lines slings (Dynema white line) to the external vacuum pouch holes
Cut (small cut) the welded holes
Vacuum the pouch
Place a foam on the container rolling bands (Version 1)
Close the container and secure with closing straps
Glue the 2 hinges on the rolling bands
Fill in the liferaft label (on container)
